## Combo 2855-5982
Cards: Dragon Tempest + Ancient Gold Dragon
Color identity: RW | Bracket: 3-4+ (Spicy) | Popularity: 29000 | Commander: legal
Produces: Near-infinite damage
Start: Dragon Tempest (battlefield); Ancient Gold Dragon (battlefield)
Prerequisites: Ancient Gold Dragon does not have summoning sickness and cannot be blocked by an opponent.
Steps: Deal combat damage using Ancient Gold Dragon.
  Ancient Gold Dragon triggers, causing you to roll a d20 and create that many 1/1 Faerie Dragon creature tokens.
  When the Faerie Dragons enter the battlefield, Dragon Tempest's second ability triggers that many times.
  Resolve the first trigger, dealing damage to any target equal to the number of Dragons you control.
  Repeat step 4 for each remaining trigger.
Notes: The amount of damage this combo deals is highly variable by how many Dragons you control and the result of the d20 roll. The more Dragons you control, the more damage will be dealt. It can also be repeated, if needed.

Color identity: RW | Bracket: 1+ (Exhibition) | Popularity: 2 | Commander: legal
Produces: Infinite artifact ETB; Infinite artifact LTB; Infinite artifact sacrifice triggers; Infinite creature LTB; Infinite creature ETB; Infinite creature sacrifice triggers; Infinite death triggers; Infinite storm count; Infinitely powerful creature until end of turn
Start: Teshar, Ancestor's Apostle (battlefield); Scrap Trawler (graveyard); Krark-Clan Grunt (battlefield); Tormod's Crypt (hand)
Steps: Cast Tormod's Crypt by paying its mana cost.
  Teshar triggers, returning Scrap Trawler from your graveyard to the battlefield.
  Activate Tormod's Crypt by tapping and sacrificing it.
  Activate Krark-Clan Grunt by sacrificing Scrap Trawler.
  When Scrap Trawler dies, it triggers, returning Tormod's Crypt from your graveyard to your hand.
  Resolve the Krark-Clan Grunt ability.
  Repeat.

## Combo 3213-3587-7711

Color identity: RW | Bracket: 1+ (Exhibition) | Popularity: 20 | Commander: legal
Produces: Infinite damage to one opponent; Infinite +1/+1 counters on a creature
Start: Red Hulk (battlefield); Boromir, Warden of the Tower (battlefield); Boros Reckoner (battlefield)
Prerequisites: You have a way to deal damage to Red Hulk.
Steps: Give Red Hulk and Boros Reckoner indestructible using Boromir.
  Deal damage to Red Hulk.
  Red Hulk triggers, putting a +1/+1 counter on it and dealing damage to Boros Reckoner.
  Boros Reckoner triggers, dealing damage to Red Hulk.
  Repeat from step 3.
Notes: The final instance of damage can be flung at an opponent for infinite damage. If you can deal more damage to Red Hulk afterwards, you can deal infinite damage to more targets.

## Combo 3214-4090
Cards: Firedrinker Satyr + Pariah
Color identity: RW | Bracket: 3-4+ (Spicy) | Popularity: 30 | Commander: legal | Mana needed: {1}{R}
Produces: Draw the game
Start: Firedrinker Satyr (battlefield); Pariah (battlefield: attached to Firedrinker Satyr)
Prerequisites: Firedrinker Satyr has indestructible.
Steps: Activate Firedrinker Satyr by paying {1}{R}, attempting to deal 1 damage to you, but dealing 1 damage to Firedrinker Satyr instead due to Pariah.
  Firedrinker Satyr triggers, attempting to deal 1 damage to you, but dealing 1 damage to Firedrinker Satyr instead due to Pariah.
  Repeat step 2 in an infinite loop, which causes the game to end in a draw.

## Combo 3232-3292-4050-4763
Cards: Koll, the Forgemaster + Phyrexian Altar + Obsidian Battle-Axe + Spear Spewer
Color identity: RW | Bracket: 1+ (Exhibition) | Popularity: 0 | Commander: legal | Mana needed: {R}
Produces: Infinite damage to all players; Near-infinite death triggers; Near-infinite creature ETB; Near-infinite creature LTB; Near-infinite creature sacrifice triggers; Near-infinite storm count
Start: Koll, the Forgemaster (battlefield); Phyrexian Altar (battlefield); Obsidian Battle-Axe (battlefield); Spear Spewer (hand)
Prerequisites: Your life total is greater than an opponent's.
Steps: Cast Spear Spewer by paying {R}.
  Spear Spewer enters the battlefield, triggering Obsidian Battle-Axe, attaching it to Spear Spewer and giving it haste.
  Activate Spear Spewer by tapping it, dealing 1 damage to each player.
  Activate Phyrexian Altar by sacrificing Spear Spewer, adding {R}.
  Koll triggers, returning Spear Spewer from your graveyard to your hand.
  Repeat until all opponents with a lower life total than your own have lost the game.

## Combo 3232-3530-4050-4763
Cards: Koll, the Forgemaster + Phyrexian Altar + Obsidian Battle-Axe + Grim Initiate
Color identity: RW | Bracket: 1+ (Exhibition) | Popularity: 0 | Commander: legal | Mana needed: {R}
Produces: Infinite colored mana; Infinitely large creature; Infinite death triggers; Infinite creature ETB; Infinite creature LTB; Infinite creature sacrifice triggers; Infinite storm count
Start: Koll, the Forgemaster (battlefield); Phyrexian Altar (battlefield); Obsidian Battle-Axe (battlefield); Grim Initiate (hand)
Steps: Cast Grim Initiate by paying {R}.
  Grim Initiate enters the battlefield, triggering Obsidian Battle-Axe, attaching it to Grim Initiate.
  Activate Phyrexian Altar by sacrificing Grim Initiate, adding {R}.
  Grim Initiate dies, triggering itself and Koll.
  Resolve Grim Initiate's trigger, amassing 1.
  Resolve Koll's trigger, returning Grim Initiate from your graveyard to your hand.
  Activate Phyrexian Altar by sacrificing the Zombie Army token, adding one mana of any color.
  Repeat.
  Once you have infinite mana, you may repeat the loop without sacrificing the Zombie Army token.

## Combo 3259-3263-3480-5953
Cards: Aya of Alexandria + Breath of Fury + Mycosynth Lattice + Purphoros, Bronze-Blooded
Color identity: RW | Bracket: 1+ (Exhibition) | Popularity: 0 | Commander: legal
Produces: Infinite combat phases; Infinite mana creatures you control can produce; Infinite combat damage; Infinite creature LTB; Infinite creature ETB; Infinite creature sacrifice triggers; Infinite death triggers; Infinite untap of creatures you control
Start: Aya of Alexandria (battlefield); Breath of Fury (battlefield); Mycosynth Lattice (battlefield); Purphoros, Bronze-Blooded (battlefield)
Prerequisites: Breath of Fury attached to any other creature you control.
  An opponent cannot block creatures you control.
Steps: Deal combat damage using the creature that has Breath of Fury attached. 
  Breath of Fury and Aya of Alexandria trigger.
  Resolve the Aya of Alexandria trigger, creating a 1/1 Assassin creature token.
  Resolve the Breath of Fury trigger, causing you to sacrifice the creature it is attached to, then attach Breath of Fury to the Assassin token, then causing you to untap all creatures you control and to get an additional combat phase after this one.
  Repeat each combat phase.

## Combo 3259-3480-4053-7604
Cards: Excava, the Risen Past + Solemnity + Breath of Fury + Purphoros, Bronze-Blooded
Color identity: RW | Bracket: 1+ (Exhibition) | Popularity: 0 | Commander: legal
Produces: Infinite combat damage; Infinite combat phases; Infinite mana creatures you control can produce; Infinite creature ETB; Infinite creature LTB; Infinite death triggers; Infinite creature sacrifice triggers; Infinite untap of creatures you control
Start: Excava, the Risen Past (battlefield); Solemnity (battlefield); Breath of Fury (battlefield); Purphoros, Bronze-Blooded (battlefield)
Prerequisites: Breath of Fury attached to another creature you control with mana value 3 or less.
  You either control another nontoken creature with mana value 3 or less, or have a creature card with mana value 3 or less in your graveyard.
  An opponent cannot block creatures you control.
Steps: Declare Excava and the creature with Breath of Fury attached as attackers.
  Excava triggers, returning up to one creature with mana value 3 or less from your graveyard to the battlefield. It won't enter with a finality counter on it due to Solemnity.
  Deal combat damage using the creature that has Breath of Fury attached. 
  Breath of Fury triggers, causing you to sacrifice the creature it is attached to, then attach Breath of Fury to another creature with mana value 3 or less, then causing you to untap all creatures you control and to get an additional combat phase after this one. 
  Repeat each combat phase.
Notes: If both creatures used have haste, you can ignore the haste enabler.

  Excava triggers, returning up to one creature with mana value 3 or less from your graveyard to the battlefield. It won't enter with a finality counter on it due to Solemnity.
  Deal combat damage using the creature that has Breath of Fury attached. 
  Breath of Fury triggers, causing you to sacrifice the creature it is attached to, then attach Breath of Fury to another creature with mana value 3 or less, then causing you to untap all creatures you control and to get an additional combat phase after this one. 
  Repeat each combat phase.
Notes: If both creatures used have haste, you can ignore the haste enabler.

## Combo 3259-4068-4302-4509

  When the Angel enters, Bishop of Wings' first ability triggers, causing you to gain 4 life.
  Repeat each combat phase.

Color identity: RW | Bracket: 1+ (Exhibition) | Popularity: 0 | Commander: legal
Produces: Infinite combat damage; Infinite combat phases; Infinite mana creatures you control can produce; Infinite creature tokens with haste; Infinite creature ETB; Infinite creature LTB; Infinite death triggers; Infinite creature sacrifice triggers; Infinite untap of creatures you control
Start: Breath of Fury (battlefield: attached to another creature you control); Tuktuk Rubblefort (battlefield); Royal Talon Fighter Jet (battlefield: with at least one +1/+1 counter on it)
Prerequisites: Royal Talon Fighter Jet is a creature.
  An opponent cannot block creatures you control.
Steps: Declare the creature with Breath of Fury attached and Royal Talon Fighter Jet as attackers.
  Royal Talon Fighter Jet triggers, creating at least one creature token.
  Deal combat damage using the creature that has Breath of Fury attached. 
  Breath of Fury triggers, causing you to sacrifice the creature it is attached to, then attach Breath of Fury to the creature token, then causing you to untap all creatures you control and to get an additional combat phase after this one.
  Repeat each combat phase.

## Combo 3259-4885-7796
Cards: Breath of Fury + Tuktuk Rubblefort + Precinct Captain
Color identity: RW | Bracket: 1+ (Exhibition) | Popularity: 0 | Commander: legal
Produces: Infinite combat damage; Infinite combat phases; Infinite mana creatures you control can produce; Infinite creature ETB; Infinite creature LTB; Infinite death triggers; Infinite creature sacrifice triggers; Infinite untap of creatures you control
Start: Breath of Fury (battlefield: attached to any other creature you control ); Tuktuk Rubblefort (battlefield); Precinct Captain (battlefield)
Prerequisites: At least one opponent cannot block creatures you control.
Steps: Deal combat damage using the creature that has Breath of Fury attached and Precinct Captain, triggering Breath of Fury and Precinct Captain.
  Resolve the Precinct Captain trigger, creating a creature token.
  Resolve the Breath of Fury trigger, causing you to sacrifice the creature it is attached to, then attach Breath of Fury to a creature token, then causing you to untap all creatures you control and to get an additional combat phase after this one. 
  Repeat each combat phase.

## Combo 3259-4885-7917--185
Cards: Breath of Fury + Seluma, Light of Aysen + Tuktuk Rubblefort
Color identity: RW | Bracket: 1+ (Exhibition) | Popularity: 0 | Commander: legal
Produces: Infinite combat damage; Infinite combat phases; Infinite mana creatures you control can produce; Infinite creature ETB; Infinite creature LTB; Infinite death triggers; Infinite creature sacrifice triggers; Infinite untap of creatures you control
Requires: 2x Angel Creature (battlefield, graveyard)
Start: Breath of Fury (battlefield: attached to the other Angel on the battlefield); Seluma, Light of Aysen (battlefield); Tuktuk Rubblefort (battlefield)
Prerequisites: An opponent cannot block creatures you control.
Steps: Deal combat damage using the creature that has Breath of Fury attached and Seluma, triggering both Breath of Fury and Seluma.
  Resolve the Seluma trigger, returning an Angel from your graveyard to the battlefield.
  Resolve the Breath of Fury trigger, causing you to sacrifice the creature it is attached to, then attach Breath of Fury to the Angel returned in step 2, then causing you to untap all creatures you control and to get an additional combat phase after this one.
  Repeat each combat phase.
Notes: One of the two additional Angels must be on the battlefield with the other in your graveyard. If both chosen Angels have haste, then Tuktuk Rubblefort can be omitted.

## Combo 3259-5233-6628--85--106
Cards: Breath of Fury + Boros Garrison + PuPu UFO
Color identity: RW | Bracket: 1+ (Exhibition) | Popularity: 11 | Commander: legal
Produces: Infinite combat damage; Infinite combat phases; Infinite mana creatures you control can produce; Infinite creature ETB; Infinite creature LTB; Infinite death triggers; Infinite landfall triggers; Infinite creature sacrifice triggers; Infinite untap of creatures you control
Requires: Haste Enabler (battlefield)
Requires: Permanent with Landfall Ability: Create Creature Token(s) (battlefield)
Start: Breath of Fury (battlefield: attached to any additional creature without summoning sickness you control); Boros Garrison (hand); PuPu UFO (battlefield: without summoning sickness)
Prerequisites: An opponent is unable to block and kill creatures you control.
Steps: Activate PuPu UFO by tapping it, putting Boros Garrison from your hand onto the battlefield.
  Boros Garrison enters the battlefield, triggering itself and the permanent with a landfall ability.
  Resolve the Boros Garrison trigger, returning it from the battlefield to your hand.
  Resolve the landfall trigger, creating a creature token.
  Deal combat damage to an opponent with the creature that has Breath of Fury attached.
  Breath of Fury triggers, causing you to sacrifice the creature it is attached to and then attach Breath of Fury to the creature token from step 4, untap all creatures you control, and get an additional combat phase after this one.
  Repeat each combat phase.

Breath of Fury triggers, causing you to sacrifice the creature it is attached to, then attach Breath of Fury to the creature token, then causing you to untap all creatures you control and to get an additional combat phase after this one. 
  Repeat each combat phase.
Notes: If you control a commander, you can choose both of SOLDIER Military Program's modes.

## Combo 3259-6791-7917--185
Cards: Breath of Fury + Seluma, Light of Aysen + Tannuk, Steadfast Second
Color identity: RW | Bracket: 1+ (Exhibition) | Popularity: 0 | Commander: legal
Produces: Infinite combat damage; Infinite combat phases; Infinite mana creatures you control can produce; Infinite creature ETB; Infinite creature LTB; Infinite death triggers; Infinite creature sacrifice triggers; Infinite untap of creatures you control
Requires: 2x Angel Creature (battlefield, graveyard)
Start: Breath of Fury (battlefield: attached to the other Angel on the battlefield); Seluma, Light of Aysen (battlefield); Tannuk, Steadfast Second (battlefield)
Prerequisites: An opponent cannot block creatures you control.
Steps: Deal combat damage using the creature that has Breath of Fury attached and Seluma, triggering both Breath of Fury and Seluma.
  Resolve the Seluma trigger, returning an Angel from your graveyard to the battlefield.
  Resolve the Breath of Fury trigger, causing you to sacrifice the creature it is attached to, then attach Breath of Fury to the Angel returned in step 2, then causing you to untap all creatures you control and to get an additional combat phase after this one.
  Repeat each combat phase.
Notes: One of the two additional Angels must be on the battlefield with the other in your graveyard. If both chosen Angels have haste, then Tannuk can be omitted.

## Combo 3839-4344-4846
Cards: Worldgorger Dragon + Changeling Hero + Lumbering Battlement
Color identity: RW | Bracket: 1+ (Exhibition) | Popularity: 0 | Commander: legal | Mana needed: {0} plus the mana cost of the creature in your hand
Produces: Infinite mana lands you control can produce; Infinite creature ETB; Infinite creature LTB; Infinite blinking of permanents
Start: Worldgorger Dragon (hand, battlefield, exile: Exiled by the creature on the battlefield); Changeling Hero (hand, battlefield, exile: Exiled by the creature on the battlefield); Lumbering Battlement (hand, battlefield, exile: Exiled by the creature on the battlefield)
Prerequisites: Lumbering Battlement must be exiled by Changeling Hero, and Worldgorger Dragon must begin in hand. (This starting order is not fixed, and their initial positions may be interchanged as necessary.)
Steps: Cast Worldgorger Dragon for its mana cost.
  When Worldgorger Dragon enters, it exiles Changeling Hero until Worldgorger Dragon leaves the battlefield. Resolve any additional effects of Worldgorger Dragon's ETB.
  When Changeling Hero leaves the battlefield, Lumbering Battlement is returned from exile to the battlefield.
  When Lumbering Battlement enters, it exiles Worldgorger Dragon until Lumbering Battlement leaves the battlefield. Resolve any additional effects of Lumbering Battlement's ETB.
  When Worldgorger Dragon leaves the battlefield, Changeling Hero is returned from exile to the battlefield.
  When Changeling Hero enters, it exiles Lumbering Battlement until Changeling Hero leaves the battlefield. Resolve any additional effects of Changeling Hero's ETB.
  When Lumbering Battlement leaves the battlefield, Worldgorger Dragon is returned from exile to the battlefield.
  Repeat from step 2.
Notes: Due to differences in card templating between the various banisher effects (linked triggered abilities vs "until" clause), steps 3, 5, and 7 may return the creature to the battlefield during the resolution of the preceding step rather than creating a separate event. This should not affect the overall execution of the combo, only how many objects pass through the stack (and thus rounds of priority available per loop).

  When a token enters, Spiritcall Enthusiast triggers, becoming prepared.

## Combo 4344-4846-5887
Cards: Worldgorger Dragon + Lumbering Battlement + Werefox Bodyguard
Color identity: RW | Bracket: 1+ (Exhibition) | Popularity: 5 | Commander: legal | Mana needed: {0} plus the mana cost of the creature in your hand
Produces: Infinite mana lands you control can produce; Infinite creature ETB; Infinite creature LTB; Infinite blinking of permanents
Start: Worldgorger Dragon (hand, battlefield, exile: Exiled by the creature on the battlefield); Lumbering Battlement (hand, battlefield, exile: Exiled by the creature on the battlefield); Werefox Bodyguard (hand, battlefield, exile: Exiled by the creature on the battlefield)
Prerequisites: Werefox Bodyguard must be exiled by Lumbering Battlement, and Worldgorger Dragon must begin in hand. (This starting order is not fixed, and their initial positions may be interchanged as necessary.)
Steps: Cast Worldgorger Dragon for its mana cost.
  When Worldgorger Dragon enters, it exiles Lumbering Battlement until Worldgorger Dragon leaves the battlefield. Resolve any additional effects of Worldgorger Dragon's ETB.

  When Werefox Bodyguard enters, it exiles Worldgorger Dragon until Werefox Bodyguard leaves the battlefield. Resolve any additional effects of Werefox Bodyguard's ETB.
  When Worldgorger Dragon leaves the battlefield, Lumbering Battlement is returned from exile to the battlefield.
  When Lumbering Battlement enters, it exiles Werefox Bodyguard until Lumbering Battlement leaves the battlefield. Resolve any additional effects of Lumbering Battlement's ETB.
  When Werefox Bodyguard leaves the battlefield, Worldgorger Dragon is returned from exile to the battlefield.
  Repeat from step 2.
Notes: Due to differences in card templating between the various banisher effects (linked triggered abilities vs "until" clause), steps 3, 5, and 7 may return the creature to the battlefield during the resolution of the preceding step rather than creating a separate event. This should not affect the overall execution of the combo, only how many objects pass through the stack (and thus rounds of priority available per loop).

## Combo 4846-5887-6966
Cards: Worldgorger Dragon + Werefox Bodyguard + All-Fates Stalker
Color identity: RW | Bracket: 1+ (Exhibition) | Popularity: 10 | Commander: legal | Mana needed: {0} plus the mana cost of the creature in your hand
Produces: Infinite mana lands you control can produce; Infinite creature ETB; Infinite creature LTB; Infinite blinking of permanents
Start: Worldgorger Dragon (hand, battlefield, exile: Exiled by the creature on the battlefield); Werefox Bodyguard (hand, battlefield, exile: Exiled by the creature on the battlefield); All-Fates Stalker (hand, battlefield, exile: Exiled by the creature on the battlefield)
Prerequisites: All-Fates Stalker must be exiled by Werefox Bodyguard, and Worldgorger Dragon must begin in hand. (This starting order is not fixed, and their initial positions may be interchanged as necessary.)
Steps: Cast Worldgorger Dragon for its mana cost.
  When Worldgorger Dragon enters, it exiles Werefox Bodyguard until Worldgorger Dragon leaves the battlefield. Resolve any additional effects of Worldgorger Dragon's ETB.
  When Werefox Bodyguard leaves the battlefield, All-Fates Stalker is returned from exile to the battlefield.
  When All-Fates Stalker enters, it exiles Worldgorger Dragon until All-Fates Stalker leaves the battlefield. Resolve any additional effects of All-Fates Stalker's ETB.
  When Worldgorger Dragon leaves the battlefield, Werefox Bodyguard is returned from exile to the battlefield.
  When Werefox Bodyguard enters, it exiles All-Fates Stalker until Werefox Bodyguard leaves the battlefield. Resolve any additional effects of Werefox Bodyguard's ETB.
  When All-Fates Stalker leaves the battlefield, Worldgorger Dragon is returned from exile to the battlefield.
  Repeat from step 2.
Notes: Due to differences in card templating between the various banisher effects (linked triggered abilities vs "until" clause), steps 3, 5, and 7 may return the creature to the battlefield during the resolution of the preceding step rather than creating a separate event. This should not affect the overall execution of the combo, only how many objects pass through the stack (and thus rounds of priority available per loop).

## Combo 5089-5540-7512
Cards: Zidane, Tantalus Thief + Confusion in the Ranks + Karn, the Great Creator
Color identity: RW | Bracket: 3-4+ (Spicy) | Popularity: 6 | Commander: legal
Produces: Infinite Treasure tokens; Infinite colored mana; Infinite artifact tokens; Infinite artifact ETB; Infinite artifact LTB; Infinite artifact sacrifice triggers; Infinite Treasure tokens for one or more opponents; Infinite artifact ETB for one or more opponents
Start: Zidane, Tantalus Thief (battlefield); Confusion in the Ranks (battlefield); Karn, the Great Creator (battlefield)
Prerequisites: You have a way to cause an artifact, creature, or enchantment to enter the battlefield.
  An opponent controls an artifact.
  If you are going to have a nonartifact creature or enchantment enter the battlefield, an opponent must also control either a creature or enchantment respectively.
Steps: Cause an artifact, creature or enchantment to enter the battlefield.
  Confusion in the Ranks triggers, exchanging control of the permanent that entered with an opponent for another permanent of the same type.
  Zidane triggers, creating a Treasure token.
  When the Treasure enters, Confusion in the Ranks triggers, exchanging control of the Treasure with an opponent for another artifact.
  Repeat from step 3.
Notes: Karn, the Great Creator prevents the opponents from sacrificing the Treasures for mana. Without it, your opponents can sacrifice the Treasures and stop you (eventually) from exchanging control of artifacts.

## Combo 5540-7000-7512

Color identity: RW | Bracket: 3-4+ (Spicy) | Popularity: 0 | Commander: legal
Produces: Infinite Treasure tokens; Infinite colored mana; Infinite artifact tokens; Infinite artifact ETB; Infinite artifact LTB; Infinite artifact sacrifice triggers; Infinite Treasure tokens for one or more opponents; Infinite artifact ETB for one or more opponents
Start: Zidane, Tantalus Thief (battlefield); Confusion in the Ranks (battlefield); Clarion Conqueror (battlefield)
Prerequisites: You have a way to cause an artifact, creature, or enchantment to enter the battlefield.
  An opponent controls an artifact.
  If you are going to have a nonartifact creature or enchantment enter the battlefield, an opponent must also control either a creature or enchantment respectively.
Steps: Cause an artifact, creature or enchantment to enter the battlefield.
  Confusion in the Ranks triggers, exchanging control of the permanent that entered with an opponent for another permanent of the same type.
  Zidane triggers, creating a Treasure token.
  When the Treasure enters, Confusion in the Ranks triggers, exchanging control of the Treasure with an opponent for another artifact.
  Repeat from step 3.
Notes: Clarion Conqueror prevents the opponents from sacrificing the Treasures for mana. Without it, your opponents can sacrifice the Treasures and stop you (eventually) from exchanging control of artifacts.
